steps)`):null),t.steps?z(\"p\",{class:\"explain\",html:`<b>${rp(t.steps.title)}.</b> ${t.steps.takeaway.html}`}):null,t.watch_for.length?z(\"ul\",{class:\"watch\",\"aria-label\":\"Watch for\"},...t.watch_for.map(n=>z(\"li\",{},z(\"span\",{},n.description,n.yours?z(\"span\",{class:\"yours\"},\" \\xB7 in your report\"):null)))):null)}function BP(e){let t=e.questions;if(t.length===0)return z(\"section\",{},z(\"h2\",{},\"Prove it\"),z(\"p\",{class:\"explain\"},\"No verified questions for this topic yet, so tonight counts as studied, not proven. Ask Claude to finish the mission when you've gone through the recap and explainer.\"),e.result?zw(e.result,e):null);let r=t[In],n=z(\"div\",{class:\"dashes\",\"aria-hidden\":\"true\"},...t.map((o,s)=>z(\"i\",{class:o.answered?o.answered.correct?\"right\":\"wrong\":s===In?\"on\":\"\"})));return z(\"section\",{\"aria-labelledby\":\"prove-h\"},z(\"div\",{class:\"qhead\"},z(\"h2\",{id:\"prove-h\"},`Prove it \\xB7 question ${In+1} of ${t.length}`),n),z(\"p\",{class:\"stem\",id:\"stem\",html:r.stem.html}),r.kind===\"mcq\"?JP(r):KP(r),r.answered?WP(r.answered,r):null,GP(r,e),jr?z(\"p\",{class:\"feedback bad\",role:\"alert\"},jr):null,e.result&&$w(e)&&In===t.length-1?zw(e.result,e):null)}var $w=e=>e.questions.every(t=>t.answered);function JP(e){let t=e.answered;return z(\"div\",{class:\"options\",role:\"group\",\"aria-labelledby\":\"stem\"},...(e.options??[]).map(r=>{let n=t?r.id===t.correct_option_id?\" is-right\":r.id===t.chosen&&!t.correct?\" is-wrong\":\"\":\"\";return KP(e){let t=z(\"input\",{id:\"num\",inputmode:\"decimal\",autocomplete:\"off\",\"aria-labelledby\":\"stem\",value:e.answered?.chosen??St??\"\",disabled:!!e.answered||On,oninput:r=>{St=r.target.value;let n=document.getElementById(\"check\");n&&(n.disabled=!St?.trim())},onkeydown:r=>{r.key===\"Enter\"&&St?.trim()&&xw(e)}});return z(\"div\",{class:\"num\"},t,e.unit?z(\"span\",{class:\"muted\"},e.unit):null)}function WP(e,t){return z(\"div\",{class:`feedback${e.correct?\"\":\" bad\"}`,role:\"status\",id:\"fb\",tabindex:\"-1\"},z(\"strong\",{},e.correct?\"Correct\":\"Not quite\"),z(\"div\",{html:e.feedback.html}),!e.correct&&e.correct_display?z(\"div\",{html:`Correct answer: ${e.correct_display.html}`}):null,e.misconception?.yours?z(\"span\",{class:\"tag\"},`Same mistake as ${e.misconception.yours.exam_name} ${e.misconception.yours.question_ref} in your report`):null,z(\"details\",{},z(\"summary\",{},\"Worked solution\"),z(\"p\",{html:e.solution.html})),e.correct?null:z(\"div\",{class:\"actions\"},z(\"button\",{class:\"link\",type:\"button\",onclick:()=>s_(`Why was my answer to this prove-it question wrong? (question_id ${t.id})`)},\"Ask Claude why \\u2192\")))}function GP(e,t){return e.answered?In<t.questions.length-1?z(\"div\",{class:\"actions\"},z(\"button\",{class:\"btn primary\",id:\"next\",type:\"button\",onclick:()=>{In++,St=void 0,jr=void 0,Kc(\"#stem\")}},\"Next question \\u2192\")):null:z(\"div\",{class:\"actions\"},z(\"button\",{class:\"btn primary\",id:\"check\",type:\"button\",disabled:On||!St?.trim(),onclick:()=>void xw(e)},On?\"Checking\\u2026\":\"Check answer\"))}function zw(e,t){let r=t.questions.filter(n=>n.answered&&!n.answered.correct).length;return z(\"div\",{class:\"feedback result\",id:\"result\",tabindex:\"-1\",style:\"background:var(--thread-soft);border-color:var(--line)\"},z(\"div\",{class:\"big\"},e.passed?\"Mission passed \\u2713\":\"Not proven yet: it's in your plan again\"),...e.outcomes.map(n=>z(\"p\",{style:\"margin:4px 0\"},z(\"b\",{},n.concept_name),`: ${n.total>0?`${n.correct}/${n.total} \\xB7 `:\"\"}${n.before} \\u2192 ${n.after}. `,z(\"span\",{class:\"muted\"},n.reason))),ww(e.readiness_after,e.readiness_before),e.ripple_root?z(\"div\",{class:\"ripple\"},z(\"b\",{},`${e.ripple_root} is fixed.`),\" Everything built on it lights up in your constellation.\"):null,z(\"div\",{class:\"actions\"},r>0?z(\"button\",{class:\"btn primary\",type:\"button\",onclick:()=>s_(`I just finished tonight's Progresso check and got ${r} wrong. Can you go over what I got wrong?`)},\"Go over my mistakes with Claude\"):z(\"button\",{class:\"btn primary\",type:\"button\",onclick:()=>s_(\"I passed tonight's Progresso check. What's next?\")},\"What's next?\"),z(\"button\",{class:\"btn\",type:\"button\",onclick:np(t.links.constellation)},\"Open my constellation\")))}function Rw(e){return z(\"div\",{class:\"foot\"},z(\"span\",{},e.next?`Next: ${e.next.title} \\xB7 ${e.next.date}`:`Today: ${e.today}`),z(\"button\",{class:\"link\",type:\"button\",onclick:np(e.links.mission??e.links.today)},\"Open in Progresso \\u2197\"))}async function xw(e){if(!(!gt||On||!St?.trim())){On=!0,jr=void 0,Kc();try{let t=await op.callServerTool({name:\"submit_answer\",arguments:{student:gt.student_id,mission_id:gt.mission.id,question_id:e.id,answer:St.trim()}});if(t.isError)jr=t.content?.find(r=>r.type===\"text\")?.text??\"Couldn't record that answer.\";else{let r=t.structuredContent;e.answered=r.feedback,r.result&&(gt.result=r.result),St=void 0,kw()}}catch(t){jr=\"Couldn't reach Progresso. Try again.\",console.error(t)}finally{On=!1,Kc(jr?void 0:gt?.result&&$w(gt)?\"#result\":\"#fb\")}}}async function kw(){let e=gt;if(!e?.mission)return;let t=e.questions.filter(s=>s.answered),r=e.questions.map((s,i)=>{let a=s.answered;if(!a)return`- Q${i+1} (question_id ${s.id}): not answered yet. ${s.stem.text}`;let c=a.misconception?` Misconception shown: \"${a.misconception.description}\"${a.misconception.yours?` (the same one their report found in ${a.misconception.yours.exam_name} ${a.misconception.yours.question_ref})`:\"\"}.`:\"\";return`- Q${i+1} (question_id ${s.id}): ${s.stem.text} \\u2192 answered ${a.chosen}: ${a.correct?\"correct\":\"wrong\"}.${c}`}),n=e.result,o=`---\nprogresso-mission: ${e.mission.id}\nstudent: ${e.student_id}\nanswered: ${t.length}/${e.questions.length}\ncorrect: ${t.filter(s=>s.answered.correct).length}\n---\nThe student is answering tonight's prove-it questions in the Progresso widget (\"${e.mission.title}\"). Their answers are already saved; don't ask them to repeat them.\n${r.join(`\n`)}${n?`\nMission finished: ${n.passed?\"passed\":\"not proven yet (it comes back in the plan)\"}. ${n.outcomes.map(s=>`${s.concept_name}: ${s.before} \\u2192 ${s.after}`).join(\"; \")}.${n.ripple_root?` The root \"${n.ripple_root}\" is now proven.`:\"\"} Readiness estimate now ${Math.round(n.readiness_after.low)}\\u2013${Math.round(n.readiness_after.high)}%.`:\"\"}\nFor any wrong answer, call explain_my_mistake with its question_id to tie it to the student's report before explaining.`;try{await op.updateModelContext({content:[{type:\"text\",text:o}]})}catch(s){console.warn(\"[progresso widget] updateModelContext not supported\",s)}}function s_(e){kw().finally(()=>{op.sendMessage({role:\"user\",content:[{type:\"text\",text:e}]}).catch(t=>console.warn(\"[progresso widget] sendMessage failed\",t))})}function rp(e){return e.replace(/[&<>\"']/g,t=>({\"&\":\"&amp;\",\"<\":\"&lt;\",\">\":\"&gt;\",'\"':\"&quot;\",\"'\":\"&#39;\"})[t])}})();\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
    }
  ]
}
